Every word of law in this repository comes from these sources. Lexifina reads them and records each act's dated versions, and the laws that made each change, in one corpus file. This repository is built from that file.

## Dates

Each commit is dated at noon (Europe/Bucharest) on the day the change took effect. The other dates, enacted and published, are trailers.

When a law gives no date of its own, the default rule applies: Constituția României art. 78: "Legea se publică în Monitorul Oficial al României și intră în vigoare la 3 zile de la data publicării sau la o dată ulterioară prevăzută în textul ei." The corpus does not apply this rule itself: every in-force date comes from the portal's own note on the amended provision ("la DD-MM-YYYY") or from the date of the consolidated form. https://legislatie.just.ro/Public/DetaliiDocument/47355

## How the history is built

`tools/build.py` turns the corpus into this history in one pass: one commit per law, on the day it took effect. The same corpus always gives the same commit hashes. `tools/check_repo.py` reads every version back from git and compares it with the corpus. `tools/on.py` shows the law as it stood on any date. The corpus format is described in `tools/SCHEMA.md`.

- Python 3.14.0; git version 2.52.0.windows.1.

## Decisions made by hand

- Romania's Phase 1: the Constitution, the Civil Code and the Penal Code, each from the Portalul legislativ, the only official source of consolidated texts with every past consolidated form and a note on each amended provision naming the amending act and the day the change took effect.
- Every page was read from Internet Archive captures; each form's capture timestamp is pinned in its version's source_url.
- One file per article (art-NNN, Civil Code art-NNNN; Articolul 112^1 is art-112-1), one line per paragraph, alineat, letter, point or indent, the first line "# Articolul N"; the marginal title is the next line. Chapter and section headings, editorial notes (Notă), the publisher's footnotes, signatures and the amendment notes themselves are not text.
- Where a form nests an article's marginal title at the end of the article before it, or prints it between two articles, it is moved back to its own article; the Civil Code's paragraph headings ("§3. ...") and their sub-headings ("I. ...") are dropped wherever a form nests them.
- Typesetting is normalised before comparing forms: cedilla ş/ţ become comma-below ș/ț (older forms use the cedilla), no-break spaces become spaces, runs of spaces collapse, “ becomes ”, « » become „ ”, en and em dashes become hyphens, footnote markers ("*)") and a leaked link marker (<LLNK ...>) are dropped.
- Credits come only from the portal's amendment notes on the changed article: from about 2017 they read "(la DD-MM-YYYY, ... a fost modificat de ... LEGEA nr. N din ...)"; older forms print the same note, undated, after a rule of dashes. A dated note counts when its date falls after the previous version and on or before this one; an undated note counts when it is new on that article. Every dated note that explains a change carries the date of the form that first shows the change, and no note is dated after its form.
- A changed article with no such note is credited to a Constitutional Court decision only when the article's own editorial note names the decision (DECIZIA nr. N ... publicată în Monitorul Oficial ...) and its effect on the text falls in the version's interval: the day the note states, or 45 days after publication (Constituția art. 147 alin. (1)). Otherwise it stays uncredited and the builder commits it as the publisher's.
- Each version takes effect on the date of its consolidated form, which is the in-force date the notes give for the changes it carries. Forms identical to the version before them (after normalisation) add no version.
- The newest captures are from June and July 2026, so amendments in force after the newest form of each act (Civil Code 03.05.2026, Penal Code 26.04.2026) may be missing.
- Constituția: the 1991 text (DetaliiDocumentAfis/1413, in force on its approval by referendum on 8 December 1991) and the text republished on 31 October 2003 (DetaliiDocument/47355), dated 29 October 2003, the day the revision took effect. The portal holds no later form: the Constitution has not been amended since.
- Constituția 2003: the republication renumbered the articles (152 became 156), so files are keyed by each text's own numbering and many change on 29 October 2003; every change that day is credited to Legea nr. 429/2003, as the portal's note on the republished text does.
- Codul civil: the first version is the code as published in 2009 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/109884), dated 1 October 2011, followed the same day by the republished text in force from that day (form 01.10.2011); changes no article note explains are credited to Legea nr. 71/2011, which ordered the republication, as the portal's own note on the code does.
- Codul civil, 2009 text: the portal prints arts. 1.251, 2.367 and 2.374 as plain paragraphs inside the article before them ("ART.1.251", "Art. 2.367") and art. 2.655 as bare paragraphs ("ART 2.655"); each is given its own file. Art. 2.448 is run into the last paragraph of art. 2.447 mid-line, and is left as the portal prints it, so art-2448 first appears on 1 October 2011.
- Codul civil: the newest capture of the 2009 text (20251206132336) is cut off at 5 MiB, so the newest complete one (20250413082055) is used. The newest form (03.05.2026, id 309285) has no capture of its own; the act's page 109884 shows that form and was captured on 25 June 2026 (20260625171643). Page 130143 (listed as the 2011 republication) also shows the 03.05.2026 form in its capture of 7 July 2026; the two are checked to agree article by article.
- Codul civil: 1 changed article(s) with no note naming a law, committed as the publisher's: 2018-10-03 art-0253.
- Codul civil: forms with no change after normalisation, so no version: 2016-07-04 (179824), 2018-11-17 (210104), 2021-01-27 (236336), 2024-05-10 (282147), 2024-10-07 (288566).
- Codul civil: cross-check passed, the 03.05.2026 form read from two captures (109884@20260625171643 and 130143@20260707145226) is identical in all 2674 articles.

- Legea nr. 190/2018: the newest form held is that of 01.12.2019 (the act page's capture 20260202205510 lists no later form); amendments in force after it may be missing.
- Legea nr. 190/2018: the first version is the law as published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 651 of 26 July 2018 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/203151), in force on 31 July 2018: its art. 16 sets entry into force 5 days after publication. - Legea societăților nr. 31/1990: the republication of 17.11.2004 (56732) has no page of its own that shows it (its DetaliiDocument page shows the newest form), so it is read from DetaliiDocumentAfis/56732, the republished text as printed in the Monitorul Oficial (capture 20260124173136); that page carries no amendment notes.
- Legea societăților nr. 31/1990: the republication of 29.01.1998 (800) has no page of its own that shows it (its DetaliiDocument page shows the newest form), so it is read from DetaliiDocumentAfis/800, the republished text as printed in the Monitorul Oficial (capture 20251009053605); that page carries no amendment notes.
- Legea nr. 31/1990: the first version is the law as published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 126-127 of 17 November 1990 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/798), dated 17 December 1990: its art. 221 puts it in force 30 days after publication.
- Legea nr. 31/1990: the portal holds no consolidated form between 4 June 1991 and the republication of 29 January 1998 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/800), so the changes of 1991-1997 arrive together that day; those no article note explains stay uncredited, since several acts made them (the republished text's note lists Legea nr. 41/1991, Legea nr. 44/1991, OUG nr. 32/1997 and others) and the text does not say which made which.
- Legea nr. 31/1990: changes on 17 November 2004 that no article note explains are credited to Legea nr. 161/2003, whose art. XII ordered that republication, as the portal's note on the republished text says.
- Legea nr. 31/1990: one file per article as each text numbers it; the republications of 1998 and 2004 renumbered the articles, so many files change on those days.
- Legea societăților nr. 31/1990: the portal editorial notes printed as paragraphs of an article (on a Constitutional Court ruling, a rectification, a provision put back in force) are not text: 25 such paragraphs across all forms, for example «* Decretul nr. 424/1972 a fost abrogat de LEGEA Nr. 35 din 3», «* Decretul-lege nr. 54/1990 a fost abrogat de LEGEA nr. 507 », «* LEGEA nr. 507 din 12 iulie 2002 va fi abrogata de LEGEA nr».
- Legea societăților nr. 31/1990: notes dated before their form while every other note on the same amending law in that form gives the form's date (a slip on the portal); the change takes the form's date: art-185 (2020-11-04, form 2020-11-05).

- Legea nr. 188/1999: the newest form held is that of 02.08.2019 (the act page's capture 20251117041334 lists no later form); amendments in force after it may be missing.
- Legea nr. 188/1999: the republication of 22.03.2004 (50608) has no captured page of its own that shows it, so it is read from DetaliiDocumentAfis/50608, the republished text as printed in the Monitorul Oficial (capture 20260513091945); that page carries no amendment notes.
- Legea nr. 188/1999: the republication of 29.05.2007 (82411) has no captured page of its own that shows it, so it is read from DetaliiDocumentAfis/82411, the republished text as printed in the Monitorul Oficial (capture 20260712173748); that page carries no amendment notes.
- Legea nr. 188/1999: Decizia CCR nr. 1629/2009 read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/115295, capture 20251217053136): it finds art. I pct. 1-5 and 26, art. III, IV, V, VIII and annex 1 of OUG nr. 105/2009 unconstitutional; art. I pct. 3 of that ordinance inserted art. 22^1 (the portal's note on it). They ceased to have effect 45 days after publication on 14 January 2010: 28 February 2010. The form of 01.03.2010 leaves art. 22^1 out with no note; the form of 11.07.2010 prints it again in the new wording of Legea nr. 140/2010 art. I pct. 3, which is credited from its own note.
- Legea nr. 188/1999: the first version is the law as published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 600 of 8 December 1999 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/20173), dated 7 January 2000: its art. 104 alin. (1) puts it in force 30 days after publication (art. 23 on publication, shown from 7 January 2000 too). The portal holds no consolidated form before 21 April 2003, so the changes made up to then arrive together that day.
- Legea nr. 188/1999: the texts republished in Monitorul Oficial nr. 251 of 22 March 2004 (art. XXVI of Legea nr. 161/2003) and nr. 365 of 29 May 2007 (art. III of Legea nr. 251/2006), with renumbered articles, are read from their own pages (DetaliiDocumentAfis/50608 and 82411). Changes on those days that no note explains are credited to the law that ordered the republication, as the republication note says; files follow each text's own numbering, so many change on those days.
- Legea nr. 188/1999: some forms print an article as an empty article heading inside the article before it (or as a bare paragraph "ART. 29 *)"), followed by its text as paragraphs of that article; the text from the heading on is filed as the article it names: Articolul 29, Articolul 32, Articolul 34.
- Legea nr. 188/1999: the portal editorial notes printed as paragraphs of an article (on a Constitutional Court ruling, a rectification, a provision put back in force) are not text: 614 such paragraphs across all forms, for example «(la 25-05-2018 Alineatul (5) din Articolul 69, Sectiunea a 4», «* A se vedea Nota de la art. 95 în care este reprodusă opini», «* NOTA C.T.C.E. S.A. Piatra-Neamț:».
- Legea nr. 188/1999: articles a form leaves out are shown repealed on the day the repealing act took effect (on publication for an emergency ordinance, 3 days after for a law, unless the act sets its own day), credited to that act as its repeal note names it: art. 22^1, left out from the form of 01.03.2010: repealed by Decizia CCR nr. 1629/2009, in force 28.02.2010 (a Constitutional Court decision: the provisions it finds unconstitutional cease to have effect 45 days after its publication in Monitorul Oficial nr. 28 of 14 January 2010 (Constituția art. 147 alin. (1)), none having been brought into line in that time).

- Codul fiscal 2003: added 2026-11-02, one of the Portalul legislativ's consolidated forms per version, as for the other acts: 55 pages read from Internet Archive captures, each pinned in forms.json and in its version's source_url. A capture was used only if it was complete and the page showed the form its document id stands for.
- Codul fiscal 2003: the newest form held is that of 04.11.2015 (the act page's capture 20240422053527 lists no later form); amendments in force after it may be missing.
- Codul fiscal 2003: Legea nr. 163/2005 read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/62091, capture 20250813042336): approving OUG nr. 138/2004, its art. I pct. 34: "La articolul I punctul 2, articolul 92 se abrogă." (art. 92 of title III as that ordinance rewrote it); the form of 03.04.2007 leaves art. 92 out.
- Codul fiscal 2003: Legea nr. 174/2004 read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/52192, capture 20260114012741): approving OG nr. 92/2003 (Codul de procedură fiscală), its art. I pct. 144 adds to that code's art. 200 (the provisions it repeals) lit. j) "art. 246 din Legea nr. 571/2003 privind Codul fiscal"; the form of 28.05.2004 leaves art. 246 out.
- Codul fiscal 2003: Legea nr. 343/2006 read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/73981, capture 20260302004114): art. I pct. 258: "Articolul 290 se abroga."; art. V alin. (1) applies art. I from 1 January 2007. The form of 03.04.2007 leaves art. 290 out.
- Codul fiscal 2003: Legea nr. 494/2004 read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/56951, capture 20251211183826): approving OG nr. 83/2004, its single article adds to the ordinance's art. I pct. 105^2 ("Alineatul (3) al articolului 237 și articolul 238 se abrogă.", its pct. 27), pct. 108 in a new wording ("Articolul 243 se abrogă.", pct. 28) and pct. 109^1 ("Articolul 245 se abrogă.", pct. 30). The form of 07.12.2004 still prints arts. 238, 243 and 245; the next, of 03.04.2007, leaves them out.
- Codul fiscal 2003: Legea nr. 76/2010 read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/118623, capture 20251118185847): approving OUG nr. 109/2009, its art. I pct. 7 adds to the ordinance pct. 79^1 ("Titlul IV "Impozitul pe venitul microîntreprinderilor" cuprinzând articolele 103-112, se abrogă.") and pct. 15 rewrites the ordinance's art. III alin. (3) to repeal also arts. 221 and 221^2. The form of 14.05.2010 leaves arts. 103-112 (with 107^1), 221 and 221^2 out.
- Codul fiscal 2003: OG nr. 83/2004 read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/54809, capture 20251215230050): art. I pct. 66: "Articolul 102 se abrogă."; its art. IV puts it in force on 1 January 2005. The forms of 03.09.2004 to 07.12.2004 still print art. 102; the next, of 03.04.2007, leaves it out.
- Codul fiscal 2003: OUG nr. 109/2009 read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/112207, capture 20260213064150): art. III alin. (3): "Începând cu data de 1 aprilie 2010 se abrogă prevederile art. 162-175 și art. 178-206 din cap. I al titlului VII ... și prevederile art. 222-237, art. 239-242, art. 244 și 244^1 din titlul VIII ..."; Legea nr. 76/2010 (art. I pct. 15) rewrote it as "art. 162-175^4 și art. 178-206 din cap. I și ... art. 221 și 221^2 din cap. V al titlului VII", keeping 1 April 2010. The form of 01.04.2010 leaves these articles out; neither text repeals arts. 176 and 177.
- Codul fiscal 2003: OUG nr. 117/2010 read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/124883, capture 20260124171902): art. I pct. 91: "Articolul 206^59 se abrogă."; art. V alin. (1) applies it from 1 January 2011. The form of 30.12.2010 still prints art. 206^59; the next held, of 01.01.2013, leaves it out.
- Codul fiscal 2003: OUG nr. 138/2004 read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/58182, capture 20260119165916): art. I pct. 3: "Articolele 96 - 101 se abrogă."; its art. III puts it in force on 1 January 2005. The next form held after 07.12.2004, of 03.04.2007, leaves arts. 96-101 out.
- Codul fiscal 2003: OUG nr. 24/2005 read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/60368, capture 20260114210537): art. I pct. 7: "Articolul 33 se abrogă."; its art. III alin. (2) puts art. I pct. 1-21 in force on 1 May 2005. The form of 03.04.2007 leaves art. 33 out.
- Codul fiscal 2003: OUG nr. 50/2008 read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/91845, capture 20260303145934): art. 14 alin. (2): on its entry into force (1 July 2008, alin. (1)) "se abrogă art. 214^1-214^3 din Legea nr. 571/2003"; the next form held after 01.07.2007, of 01.11.2008, leaves them out.
- Codul fiscal 2003: forms listed by the portal that could not be read (in neither archive, or every capture a page without the act's text), so not versions; their changes arrive with the next form held and take its date, though their notes give the earlier day: 01.01.2015 (163935).
- Codul fiscal 2003: the first version is the code as published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 927 of 23 December 2003 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/48725), dated 1 January 2004, the day its art. 297 puts it in force. The portal holds no consolidated form between 7 December 2004 and 3 April 2007, so the changes made in those years arrive with the form of 3 April 2007, save the repeals, which are dated by their acts.
- Codul fiscal 2003: the newest form the portal holds is that of 4 November 2015. Legea nr. 227/2015, the Codul fiscal in force from 1 January 2016 (in this record as its own act), replaced it; the portal shows that as no form of this law, so the record ends with that text.
- Codul fiscal 2003: arts. 175^1-175^4 are left out from the form of 1 April 2010 and dated by OUG nr. 109/2009 art. III alin. (3) (1 April 2010), as the portal's repeal note names it; that paragraph as published names "art. 162-175", and Legea nr. 76/2010 rewrote it to name "art. 162-175^4" and arts. 221 and 221^2, keeping 1 April 2010. Arts. 221 and 221^2 stay until the form of 14 May 2010, the day Legea nr. 76/2010 took effect, and are dated by it.
- Codul fiscal 2003: the portal editorial notes printed as paragraphs of an article (on a Constitutional Court ruling, a rectification, a provision put back in force) are not text: 312 such paragraphs across all forms, for example «* Art. 2 din ORDINUL nr. 1.016 din 18 iulie 2005, publicat î», «* NOTA C.T.C.E. S.A. Piatra-Neamț:», «* NOTĂ C.T.C.E. S.A. Piatra-Neamț:».
- Codul fiscal 2003: some forms print a repealed article twice, one copy nested in the other; one copy is kept: Articolul 100, Articolul 101, Articolul 102, Articolul 67, Articolul 68, Articolul 86.

- Codul fiscal 2003: in the forms of 03.04.2007, 01.11.2008-08.12.2014 some articles are printed only with the portal's note that an earlier chapter was eliminated ("Cap. II anterior din Titlul III a fost eliminat, întrucât ... a fost înlocuit în totalitate Titlul III"), or with no text; that note is the portal's, not the law's text, so the article is read as left out of that form (851 article prints).
- Codul fiscal 2003: the forms of 03.04.2007, 01.11.2008-08.12.2014 do not print the text in force of these articles of title III, and no page held gives it: the source prints only the note on the replaced chapter in those years. From the first such form after each article's last held wording until the next form that prints it, the article reads "[Sursa nu dă textul în vigoare la această dată.]" (the source does not give the text in force on this date), committed as the publisher's; - Codul fiscal 2003: arts. 176 and 177 of title VII (the level of excise duties and the excise on cigarettes) are printed in no form of 01.04.2010-01.01.2013, and no act repealed them: OUG nr. 109/2009 art. III alin. (3), as published and as Legea nr. 76/2010 (art. I pct. 15) rewrote it, repeals arts. 162-175 (as rewritten, 162-175^4) and 178-206, not arts. 176 and 177, and OUG nr. 117/2010 (art. I pct. 86 and 87, from 1 January 2011) amends both. As for the articles of title III, from the first such form until the next form that prints them the article reads "[Sursa nu dă textul în vigoare la această dată.]" (the source does not give the text in force on this date), committed as the publisher's, and the text printed again is taken as printed: art. 176: 01.04.2010-01.01.2013 (printed again 01.02.2014); art. 177: 01.04.2010-01.01.2013 (printed again 01.02.2014).

- Legea nr. 85/2006: added 2026-11-02, one of the Portalul legislativ's consolidated forms per version, as for the other acts: 14 pages read from Internet Archive captures, each pinned in forms.json and in its version's source_url. A capture was used only if it was complete and the page showed the form its document id stands for.
- Legea nr. 85/2006: the newest form held is that of 01.02.2014 (the act page's capture 20251016165656 lists no later form); amendments in force after it may be missing.
- Legea nr. 85/2006: Legea nr. 169/2010 read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/120777, capture 20250814041911): art. I pct. 58: "Articolul 154^1 se abrogă."; the form of 24.07.2010 leaves art. 154^1 out.
- Legea nr. 85/2006: Legea nr. 277/2009 read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/109510, capture 20251007030912): approving OUG nr. 173/2008, its art. I pct. 6 rewrites that ordinance's pct. 5 as "Articolul 10 se abrogă." and pct. 10 adds pct. 14^7 "Articolul 42 se abrogă." and 14^9 "Articolul 50 se abrogă."; the form of 17.07.2009 leaves arts. 10, 42 and 50 out.
- Legea nr. 85/2006: OUG nr. 173/2008 read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/99583, capture 20251017011532): art. I pct. 22: "Articolul 155 se abrogă."; the form of 26.11.2008 leaves art. 155 out.
- Legea nr. 85/2006: the first version is the law as published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 359 of 21 April 2006 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/71011), dated 20 July 2006: its art. 156 alin. (1) puts it in force 90 days after publication. The portal holds no consolidated form before 22 November 2006, so the changes made up to then arrive together that day.
- Legea nr. 85/2006: the newest form the portal holds is that of 1 February 2014. Legea nr. 85/2014, which replaced it from 28 June 2014, is not shown as a form of this law, so the record ends with that text.
- Legea nr. 85/2006: the portal editorial notes printed as paragraphs of an article (on a Constitutional Court ruling, a rectification, a provision put back in force) are not text: 12 such paragraphs across all forms, for example «Conform art. 147 din CONSTITUȚIA ROMÂNIEI republicată în MON», «Prin urmare, în intervalul 15 ianuarie 2008-29 februarie 200», «Prin urmare, începând cu data de 15 ianuarie 2008, dispoziți».
- Legea nr. 85/2006: articles a form leaves out are shown repealed on the day the repealing act took effect (on publication for an emergency ordinance, 3 days after for a law, unless the act sets its own day), credited to that act as its repeal note names it: art. 155, left out from the form of 26.11.2008: repealed by OUG nr. 173/2008, in force 26.11.2008 (an emergency ordinance with no in-force clause of its own: in force on publication in Monitorul Oficial nr. 792 of 26 November 2008); art. 10, left out from the form of 17.07.2009: repealed by Legea nr. 277/2009, in force 17.07.2009 (a law with no in-force clause of its own: in force 3 days after publication in Monitorul Oficial nr. 486 of 14 July 2009 (Constituția art. 78)); art. 42, left out from the form of 17.07.2009: repealed by Legea nr. 277/2009, in force 17.07.2009 (a law with no in-force clause of its own: in force 3 days after publication in Monitorul Oficial nr. 486 of 14 July 2009 (Constituția art. 78)); art. 50, left out from the form of 17.07.2009: repealed by Legea nr. 277/2009, in force 17.07.2009 (a law with no in-force clause of its own: in force 3 days after publication in Monitorul Oficial nr. 486 of 14 July 2009 (Constituția art. 78)); art. 154^1, left out from the form of 24.07.2010: repealed by Legea nr. 169/2010, in force 24.07.2010 (a law with no in-force clause of its own: in force 3 days after publication in Monitorul Oficial nr. 505 of 21 July 2010 (Constituția art. 78)).
- Legea nr. 85/2006: articles already shown repealed that a later form prints again as "Abrogat" are typesetting, committed as the publisher's: 15.02.2013 art-010, 15.02.2013 art-042, 15.02.2013 art-050, 15.02.2013 art-154-1, 15.02.2013 art-155.

- Legea nr. 85/2006: forms with no change after normalisation, so no version: 2008-01-15 (95262), 2008-03-01 (95263), 2013-11-01 (172759).
- Legea nr. 215/2001: added 2026-11-02, one of the Portalul legislativ's consolidated forms per version, as for the other acts: 38 pages read from Internet Archive captures, each pinned in forms.json and in its version's source_url. A capture was used only if it was complete and the page showed the form its document id stands for.
- Legea nr. 215/2001: the newest form held is that of 20.07.2019 (the act page's capture 20251018081140 lists no later form); amendments in force after it may be missing.
- Legea nr. 215/2001: the republication of 20.02.2007 (79638) has no captured page of its own that shows it, so it is read from DetaliiDocumentAfis/79638, the republished text as printed in the Monitorul Oficial (capture 20260510115133); that page carries no amendment notes.
- Legea nr. 215/2001: Legea nr. 161/2003 read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/43323, capture 20260504205954): art. 117 (title IV): "Pe data intrării în vigoare a prezentei legi se abroga ... art. 30, art. 60 alin. (1) lit. b), art. 62, art. 72 alin. (2) lit. b), art. 131 ... din Legea administrației publice locale nr. 215/2001". The form of 21.04.2003 (the day of publication) already leaves arts. 30, 62 and 131 out; the repeal is dated 24 April 2003, and the articles stay until then.
- Legea nr. 215/2001: Legea nr. 286/2006 read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/73544, capture 20260114144002): art. I pct. 27 ("Articolele 32 și 33 se abrogă"), 50 (art. 57), 66 (arts. 74, 75 and 76), 68 (arts. 78, 79 and 81), 71 (arts. 83-86), 99 (art. 120), 106 (section 2 of chapter VIII, arts. 145-148) and 107 ("Articolele 149-153 se abrogă"); the form of 21.07.2006 leaves them out with no note.
- Legea nr. 215/2001: Legea nr. 340/2004 read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/53708, capture 20260306160438): art. 44: on its entry into force "prevederile art. 26 și 27, cele ale secțiunii 1 'Prefectul județului și al municipiului București' a cap. VIII 'Prefectul' și ale art. 154 alin. (2)" of Legea nr. 215/2001 are repealed. Section 1 of chapter VIII is arts. 130-144. The form of 06.12.2004 leaves arts. 26, 27, 130 and 132-144 out with no note.
- Legea nr. 215/2001: Legea nr. 393/2004 read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/55664, capture 20260510115133): art. 85 alin. (2) lit. a): on the same day "art. 53, art. 60, alin. (2) al art. 72, art. 80 și alin. (2) al art. 103 din Legea administrației publice locale nr. 215/2001" are repealed; the form of 06.12.2004 leaves arts. 53, 60 and 80 out with no note.
- Legea nr. 215/2001: the first version is the law as published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 204 of 23 April 2001 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/28009), dated 23 May 2001: its art. 157 alin. (1) puts it in force 30 days after publication. The portal holds no consolidated form before 29 April 2002, so the changes made up to then arrive together that day.
- Legea nr. 215/2001: the text republished in Monitorul Oficial nr. 123 of 20 February 2007 under art. III of Legea nr. 286/2006, with renumbered articles, is read from its own page (DetaliiDocumentAfis/79638). Changes on that day that no note explains are credited to Legea nr. 286/2006, as the republication note says; files follow each text's own numbering, so many change on that day.
- Legea nr. 215/2001: the portal's form dated 23 April 2004 (58825) already prints changes made by Legea nr. 141/2004 (Monitorul Oficial nr. 396 of 4 May 2004, in force 7 May 2004), the rectification in Monitorul Oficial nr. 410 of 7 May 2004 and Legea nr. 340/2004 (in force 24 July 2004): its notes name them, it leaves out arts. 26 and 27 and prints arts. 130-144 as one empty range, as Legea nr. 340/2004 art. 44 repealed them. Under the rule for text that appears before its law, in that version only the articles Legea nr. 141/2004 inserted (120^1-120^3) are absent, art. 131 (repealed in 2003, printed only inside the range) stays absent, and the other provisions concerned read "[Sursa nu dă textul în vigoare la această dată.]" (the source does not give the text in force on this date), committed as the publisher's; the form of 7 May 2004 and the later forms give their text.
- Legea nr. 215/2001: the newest form is that of 20 July 2019; the Codul administrativ (OUG nr. 57/2019, in this record as its own act) repealed the law from 5 July 2019 save the provisions it kept, and the record ends with that text.
- Legea nr. 215/2001: articles a form leaves out whose repeal applies after that form keep their text until the repealing provision takes effect, and are shown repealed on that day: art. 30, left out from the form of 21.04.2003: repealed by Legea nr. 161/2003, in force for that provision on 24.04.2003 (title IV, which holds art. 117, sets no day of its own (titles I-III and V do), so 3 days after publication in Monitorul Oficial nr. 279 of 21 April 2003 (Constituția art. 78)), so its text stays through the form(s) of 21.04.2003; art. 62, left out from the form of 21.04.2003: repealed by Legea nr. 161/2003, in force for that provision on 24.04.2003 (title IV, which holds art. 117, sets no day of its own (titles I-III and V do), so 3 days after publication in Monitorul Oficial nr. 279 of 21 April 2003 (Constituția art. 78)), so its text stays through the form(s) of 21.04.2003; art. 131, left out from the form of 21.04.2003: repealed by Legea nr. 161/2003, in force for that provision on 24.04.2003 (title IV, which holds art. 117, sets no day of its own (titles I-III and V do), so 3 days after publication in Monitorul Oficial nr. 279 of 21 April 2003 (Constituția art. 78)), so its text stays through the form(s) of 21.04.2003.
- Legea nr. 215/2001: the portal editorial notes printed as paragraphs of an article (on a Constitutional Court ruling, a rectification, a provision put back in force) are not text: 24 such paragraphs across all forms, for example «Prin ORDONANȚA DE URGENȚĂ nr. 30 din 25 aprilie 2007, public».

- Legea nr. 677/2001: the newest form held is that of 22.10.2007 (the act page's capture 20260207222925 lists no later form); amendments in force after it may be missing.
- Legea nr. 677/2001: the first version is the law as published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 790 of 12 December 2001 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/32733), in force that day (its art. 36). The portal holds two consolidated forms, of 12 May 2005 (Legea nr. 102/2005) and 22 October 2007 (Legea nr. 278/2007); the changes made before each arrive with it. The articles' marginal titles ("Definiții", "Dreptul de acces la date") are read as text, as the law prints them; the title of art. 1 ("Scop"), printed right after the chapter heading, is read only in the form of 2007, where it is the publisher's change.
- Legea nr. 677/2001: repealed in full by Legea nr. 129/2018 (enacted 15 June 2018, published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 503 of 19 June 2018), read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/201712, capture 20260214225718). Its art. V alin. (1) reads "La data de 25 mai 2018 se abrogă Legea nr. 677/2001 ..." (the day the General Data Protection Regulation applied), and the portal's note on the law gives that day. But the law was published only on 19 June 2018, and its art. VIII alin. (2) puts it in force 5 days after publication, save art. I pct. 1 and 6-11 and arts. VI and VII (from 25 May 2018); art. V is not among them. A law provides only for the future (Constituția art. 15 alin. (2)), so the repeal is dated 24 June 2018, the day art. V took effect: the record ends with a version that removes every article, credited to Legea nr. 129/2018. Legea nr. 190/2018, the GDPR implementing law (legea-190-2018/), does not mention it.

- Legea nr. 36/1995: OUG nr. 25/2005 read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/60448, capture 20260215084408): articol unic: "Articolul 31 din Legea notarilor publici și a activității notariale nr. 36/1995 ... se abrogă."
- Legea nr. 36/1995: OUG nr. 85/1997 read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/11532, capture 20260306035044): art. 44: "La data intrării în vigoare a prezentei ordonanțe de urgență se abrogă: ... art. 111 din Legea notarilor publici și a activității notariale nr. 36/1995 ..."
- Legea nr. 36/1995: the first version is the law as published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 92 of 16 May 1995 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/5944), in force that day: it sets no day of its own (Constituția art. 78 as it then stood). The portal holds no form before 31 October 2000, so the changes made up to then arrive together that day, save the repeal of art. 111 (below).
- Legea nr. 36/1995: the republications of 18 October 2011 (art. 218 of Legea nr. 71/2011), 4 February 2013 (art. VII of Legea nr. 77/2012), 18 June 2014 (art. 107 alin. (3) of Legea nr. 255/2013) and 19 March 2018 (art. IX of OUG nr. 1/2016, as introduced by Legea nr. 17/2017), each with renumbered articles, are read from their own pages (the 2018 text from the act page as captured while it showed that form); changes on those days that no note explains are credited to the act that ordered the republication, as each republication note says; files follow each text's own numbering.
- Legea nr. 36/1995: arts. 88^1-88^9 (section III^1, inserted by OUG nr. 177/2000) are printed from the form of 17 June 2003 as one range reading "Eliminate.", the portal noting that the section was removed when Legea nr. 223/2001 rejected that ordinance; that change is the publisher's, dated by the form. The forms of 4 April 2005 and 9 November 2007 leave the range out and the next forms print it again unchanged, so it is kept; the form of 25 November 2010 prints it under the misprinted title "Secțiunea 88^1-Articolul 88^9", read as the same range.
- Legea nr. 36/1995: the forms of 4 April 2005 to 25 November 2008 and of 2013-2017 print the lettered sub-headings of the section on other notarial procedures ("B. Dare de dată certă înscrisurilor", "C. Certificarea unor fapte" ... "H. Eliberarea de duplicate ale înscrisurilor notariale") as the last line of the article before; they are headings, not text, and are left out. The forms of 17 June 2003 to 25 November 2010 print a consolidator's note on the rejected OUG nr. 177/2000 inside art. 8, between its lit. c) and d); the note is left out and lit. d)-l) after it are read as text.
- Legea nr. 36/1995: the portal editorial notes printed as paragraphs of an article (on a Constitutional Court ruling, a rectification, a provision put back in force) are not text: 15 such paragraphs across all forms, for example «(la 13-03-2020 Alineatul (1) din Articolul 63, Sectiunea a 7», «(la 13-03-2020 Alineatul (4) din Articolul 63, Sectiunea a 7», «* A se vedea Ordinul ministrului justiției nr. 2.333/C/2013 ».

XIV of title XVI of Legea nr. 247/2005 (enacted 19 July 2005, published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 653 of 22 July 2005), with renumbered articles, is read from its own page (DetaliiDocumentAfis/64951); changes on that day that no note explains are credited to Legea nr. 247/2005, as the republication note says.
- Legea nr. 304/2004: the newest form, of 16 December 2022, prints every article as repealed ("Abrogat."), each note naming Legea nr. 304/2022; those changes are credited to it from the notes.

(3) al art. 64 a fost modificat de art. V din ORDONANȚ».

- Codul de procedură fiscală: added 2026-11-02, one of the Portalul legislativ's consolidated forms per version, as for the other acts. A capture was used only if it was complete and the page showed the form its document id stands for. The forms are those listed by DetaliiDocumentAfis/295429 as captured on 2026-06-18, the newest capture of any of the act's pages: 69 forms, 68 held.
- Codul de procedură fiscală: the newest form held is that of 25.02.2026; the portal lists a later form, of 08.05.2026 (309359), which neither archive holds (no capture of its own page, and no capture of the code's page 170007 made while it was the newest form), so the record ends at the newest form held and amendments in force from that day are missing.
- Codul de procedură fiscală: the first version is the code as published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 547 of 23 July 2015 (Legea nr. 207/2015, enacted 20 July 2015; DetaliiDocumentAfis/170007), dated 1 January 2016, the day its art. 353 puts it in force. It never applied in this form: OUG nr. 50/2015 amended it before that day, and the next version, the same day, is the portal's first form (01.01.2016), its changes credited from the article notes.
- Codul de procedură fiscală: the portal's page for Legea nr. 207/2015 (DetaliiDocument/170005) lists the forms but prints no text; the code's own page (DetaliiDocument/170007) and every form's page print it. The form of 03.09.2022 is page 258163.
- Codul de procedură fiscală: the forms of 06.12.2016 and 01.01.2017 print the inserted art. 163 alin. (11^1)-(11^12) between the old-style notes on them; alin. (11^5), whose text says "nu au fost confirmate de instituțiile ...", is read as text, not as a note, since it opens like a provision.
- Codul de procedură fiscală: art. 350 alin. (1) reads "Încetat efecte juridice." from the form of 16.06.2019; the change is credited to Decizia Curții Constituționale nr. 72/2019 (Monitorul Oficial nr. 332 of 2 May 2019), named in the article's editorial note, 45 days after its publication (Constituția art. 147 alin. (1)): 16 June 2019, the form's day.
- Codul de procedură fiscală: the forms of 21.01.2018 to 01.02.2022 leave out art. 80 alin. (2) and (3), inserted by OG nr. 30/2017 from 1 January 2018, and the form of 03.02.2022 prints them again unchanged, with their note of 01.01.2018; no act repealed them in between. The form of 05.05.2017 leaves out the repeal stubs art. 247 alin. (7), art. 250 alin. (4) lit. l), art. 291 alin. (5) and art. 306 alin. (4), which the form of 21.01.2018 prints again. These are the publisher's changes, committed as such and labelled unexplained.
- Codul de procedură fiscală: the form of 01.01.2016 prints, inside art. 354, a footnote marker and the notes on the EU directives that chapters I and II of title X transpose; in the code as published they follow the articles, so they are not article text and are left out.
- Codul de procedură fiscală: art. 336 changes on 7 January 2023 by its note (OUG nr. 188/2022 art. I pct. 43, lit. g)), between the forms of 29.12.2022 and 03.02.2023; that version takes the text of the form of 03.02.2023, which also prints lit. k^4) as repealed by OG nr. 16/2023 from 3 February 2023, so that repeal shows from 7 January 2023 too.
- Codul de procedură fiscală: changes whose notes give an in-force day between two forms the portal holds are dated by the note, with the later form's text for those articles: 2023-01-07 (art-336; form 03.02.2023).
- Codul de procedură fiscală: the portal editorial notes printed as paragraphs of an article (on a Constitutional Court ruling, a rectification, a provision put back in force) are not text: 168 such paragraphs across all forms, for example «(la 01-01-2018 Alineatul (8), Articolul 163 din Capitolul II», «(la 01-01-2018 Articolul 336 din Titlul XI a fost completat », «(la 01-01-2018 Articolul 80 din Capitolul VII, Titlul III a ».

- Legea nr. 126/2018: Legea nr. 158/2020 (read as published, DetaliiDocumentAfis/228486; Monitorul Oficial nr. 673 of 29 July 2020) is in force 30 days after publication, on 28 August 2020, save its art. VIII pct. 16, in force on 3 September 2020 (its art. XXVIII). Art. VIII pct. 16 rewrites art. 177 alin. (3) and (5); the portal prints the new wording from its form of 28 August 2020. Under the rule for text printed before its law, the earlier wording of both alineate, as the previous forms print it, stands until 3 September 2020, when the new wording takes effect, credited to Legea nr. 158/2020.
- Legea nr. 126/2018: Legea nr. 188/2022 (read as published, DetaliiDocumentAfis/256681; Monitorul Oficial nr. 617 of 23 June 2022) is in force 10 days after publication, 3 July 2022, the form's date; its art. (4) partea introductivă and lit. d)) to 1 January 2023 only as to the environmental objectives of art. 9 lit. c)-f) of Regulamentul (UE) 2020/852, a limit on what the provision covers, not on its wording; the new wording is shown from 3 July 2022, as the portal dates it.

- Legea nr. 143/2000: the first version is the law as published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 362 of 3 August 2000 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/23629), dated 3 August 2000: it sets no in-force day of its own, so it took effect on publication (Constituția of 1991, art. 78). The portal's first form is of 1 March 2003, so the changes made up to then arrive together that day.
- Legea nr. 143/2000: the text republished in Monitorul Oficial nr. 163 of 6 March 2014 under art. 107 alin. (3) of Legea nr. 255/2013 has no DetaliiDocument capture; it is read from its text as printed (DetaliiDocumentAfis/156116), credited to Legea nr. 255/2013 as the republication note says. It renumbers the articles, so numbers it leaves out are not repeals.
- Legea nr. 143/2000: arts. 19^1 and 19^2 were inserted by Legea nr. 522/2004 (art. I pct. 12), whose art. II puts the law in force 3 months after publication "cu excepția art. 19^1 și 19^2 din Legea nr. 143/2000, care intră în vigoare la data intrării în vigoare a noului Cod penal". The Penal Code of 2004 (Legea nr. 301/2004) never took effect (repealed by art. 446 alin. (2) of Legea nr. 286/2009); the Penal Code of 2009 took effect on 1 February 2014, the day Legea nr. 187/2012 (art. 81 pct. 11 and 12) reworded both articles. The portal prints them from its form of 7 March 2005. Under the rule for text printed before its law, both are absent until 1 February 2014, when they appear in Legea nr. 522/2004's wording, credited to it, and the same day in Legea nr. 187/2012's. (Art. 19^2 on 15 February 2010 in the list of uncredited changes below is a spelling change in the text as printed then, not shown.)
- Legea nr. 143/2000: the text republished on 6 March 2014 and the form of 8 May 2014 print the annex's tables of substances (from "TABELUL Nr. I") at the end of art. 27, with no annex markup; every other form prints them as the annex. Those lines are the annex, not art. 27's text, and are left out.
- Legea nr. 143/2000: the form of 15 February 2010 reprints older undated notes in new wording; a reworded note naming a law already noted on that article is the same note, so a typesetting change that day is not credited to it.
- Legea nr. 143/2000: the changes of 1 February 2014, the day the 2009 Penal Code and Code of Criminal Procedure took effect, are credited as the portal's notes name them: Legea nr. 187/2012 (art. 81) and Legea nr. 255/2013 (art. 43). The other changes no note explains (15 February 2010, 1 February 2014) are diacritics and spelling only, the publisher's.
- Legea nr. 143/2000: art. 12 was amended by art. 28 of Legea nr. 39/2003, in force 28 February 2003 (its art. 33); the change arrives with the portal's first form, of 1 March 2003, credited to that law.
- Legea nr. 143/2000: articles a form leaves out are shown repealed on the day the repealing act took effect (on publication for an emergency ordinance, 3 days after for a law, unless the act sets its own day), credited to that act as its repeal note names it: art. 9, left out from the form of 01.01.2007: repealed by OUG nr. 121/2006, in force 01.01.2007 (its art. 31: "Prezenta ordonanță de urgență intră în vigoare la data de 1 ianuarie 2007").
- Legea nr. 143/2000: the consolidator's notes printed inside an article ("*) NOTA C.T.C.E. S.A. Piatra-Neamţ:", quoting another act, with that act's lettered list) are not the act's text and are left out: 10 paragraphs and items across all forms.

- Legea nr. 151/2015: the law as published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 464 of 26 June 2015 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/169194). Its art. 93 puts it in force 6 months after publication, except art. 92 (setting up the insolvency commissions and the implementing rules), in force 3 days after publication. So the first version, of 29 June 2015, holds only art. 92; the rest of the law as published is added on the day it took effect, credited to Legea nr. 151/2015.
- Legea nr. 151/2015: that day was put off three times before it came, as the portal's notes on the law say: to 31 December 2016 by the single article of OUG nr. 61/2015 (Monitorul Oficial nr. 962 of 24 December 2015), to 1 August 2017 by art. IX of OUG nr. 98/2016 (Monitorul Oficial nr. 1.030 of 21 December 2016) and to 1 January 2018 by the single article of OG nr. 6/2017 (Monitorul Oficial nr. 614 of 28 July 2017). None of these acts changes the law's text; the rest of the law is shown from 1 January 2018.
- Legea nr. 151/2015: the portal's forms of 24 December 2015, 21 December 2016 and 31 July 2017 come before the law took effect and are not used. No act changed the text before then: the forms of 2015 and 2016 differ from the text as published only in one word of art. 22 alin. (1) ("le-ar" where the text as published and the later forms print "lear"), and the form of 2017 not at all. The form of 1 January 2018, the newest the portal lists, prints the law as published.

- Legea nr. 187/2012: the law as published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 757 of 12 November 2012 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/142722). Its art. 247 puts it in force on 1 February 2014, with the Penal Code, except art. 247 itself, art. 121 pct. 8 and art. 249, in force 3 days after publication. So the first version, of 15 November 2012, holds only those provisions (art. 121 with its opening words and pct. 8 alone); on 1 February 2014 the rest of the law as published is added, credited to Legea nr. 187/2012, and the same day the portal's form of 01.02.2014 gives the text in force.
- Legea nr. 187/2012: the portal's form of 25 October 2013 comes before the day the law took effect save its early provisions, and is not used; its changes (the rectification of art. 111 and typesetting, one of them in art. 249) arrive with the form of 1 February 2014. N" inside an article stay text of that article. In later forms the portal rewrites some quoted text as the amended act changed and adds a note naming that act (forms of 12.01.2015 and 30.03.2018); such a change is credited from the note: an undated note takes the date of the form that first prints it.

- Legea nr. 193/2000: the first version is the law as published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 560 of 10 November 2000 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/24922), in force 30 days after publication (its art. 17): 10 December 2000.
- Legea nr. 193/2000: the portal's first form, of 15 December 2003, prints the law with the changes of Legea nr. 65/2002 alone (its heading and every note name only that law). Legea nr. 65/2002 (Monitorul Oficial nr. 52 of 25 January 2002; read as published, DetaliiDocumentAfis/33591) has no in-force clause of its own, so it took effect on publication (Constituția of 1991, art. 78): the form's changes are dated 25 January 2002. Its art. 2 replaces "contract standard" and "clauză standard" with "contract standard preformulat" and "clauză standard preformulată" throughout the law; the form gives art. 4 no note of its own, and the change to art. 4 is credited to Legea nr. 65/2002 from that article. The form's other changes (diacritics and spacing) are the publisher's.
- Legea nr. 193/2000: the republications of 20 December 2006 (art. III of Legea nr. 65/2002) and 18 April 2008 (art. V lit. m) of title III of Legea nr. 363/2007), each with renumbered articles, are read from their own pages (DetaliiDocumentAfis/77879, 91502); changes on those days that no note explains are credited to the act that ordered the republication, as each republication note says; files follow each text's own numbering.
- Legea nr. 193/2000: the republication of 3 August 2012 (Monitorul Oficial nr. 543, under art. 80 of Legea nr. 76/2012; DetaliiDocumentAfis/140214) prints the law as art. 38 of Legea nr. 76/2012 amends it. Those amendments took effect later: art. 38 pct. 3 (the word "profesionist" for "comerciant") on 15 February 2013 and pct. 1 and 2 (arts. 12 and 13) on 1 July 2013 (art. 82 of Legea nr. 76/2012 as amended by art. I pct. 2 of OUG nr. 4/2013; both read as published, DetaliiDocumentAfis/138484 and 145075). Apart from them the republished text is the in-force text of the form of 22 July 2010: the portal's form of 31 January 2013 prints that text, with only OUG nr. 4/2013's art. 13 alin. (3) added. Under the rule for text printed before its law, the republication is not read as a version of its own (it would change nothing in force that day): the earlier text stands, and each amendment arrives on its own day with the forms of 15 February and 1 July 2013.
- Legea nr. 193/2000: the portal lists two forms of 1 July 2013. The first (DetaliiDocument/145121) prints the text of 15 February 2013 unchanged; the second (DetaliiDocument/149436) prints arts. 12 and 13 in the wording of art. 38 pct. 1 and 2 of Legea nr. 76/2012, in force that day, with no note; those two changes are credited to Legea nr. 76/2012 (read as published). That form also prints art. 4 alin. (2) with "comercianți" again (the form of 13 June 2014 has "profesioniști" back) and art. 17 with "La aceeași dată": no act made these changes, and they are the publisher's.

- Legea nr. 21/1996: the forms of 30 January 2017, 8 July 2017 and 19 April 2019 have no capture of their own page; each is read from its DetaliiDocumentAfis page, which marks that form as the one shown in its list of forms. - Legea nr. 21/1996: Legea nr. 149/2011 (art. I pct. 8) rewrites art. 17 alin. (1), but its art. II puts that alineat in force one year after the law itself (in force 14 July 2011): on 14 July 2012. The portal prints the new wording from its form of 14 July 2011. Under the rule for text printed before its law, the earlier wording of alin. (1), as the previous forms print it, stands until 14 July 2012, when the new wording takes effect, credited to Legea nr. 149/2011.
- Legea nr. 21/1996: the portal's notes on arts. 8 and 15 name orders of the Consiliul Concurenței (Ordinul nr. 13/2001 and nr. 216/2002) that raised the turnover thresholds the law lets the Council set. An order is not credited as a law: the threshold in art. 15 that changes with the form of 17 December 2002 is committed as the publisher's, and the orders' text that the forms of 17 December 2002 to 3 December 2004 quote at the end of art. 8 ("Plafonul privind cifra de afaceri ... se majorează ...") is not the law's text and is left out. The 2005, 2009 and early 2014 texts print a lone "*" as the last line of art. 67 (the opening of the closing note); it is left out.
- Legea nr. 21/1996: the forms from 6 September 2020 print the portal's notes that provisions went back to their earlier wording, were removed or were put back in force because Decizia Curții Constituționale nr. 239/2020 (Monitorul Oficial nr. 649 of 23 July 2020; read as published, DetaliiDocumentAfis/228306) found OUG nr. 39/2017 unconstitutional as a whole, the ordinance ceasing to have effect 45 days after publication, on 6 September 2020 (Constituția art. 147 alin. (1)), the form's date; the decision is credited with those changes. The form of 5 September 2009 likewise notes that art. 28 alin. (2) ceased to have effect under Decizia Curții Constituționale nr. 1.037/2009 (Monitorul Oficial nr. 501 of 21 July 2009); the decision is credited with that change, dated by the form.

Legea nr. 12/1998 (enacted 8 January 1998, published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 15 of 19 January 1998; read as published, DetaliiDocumentAfis/14026) takes effect 30 days after publication (its art. 3), and the republished text prints its changes (the republication's own note lists the amended provisions as in force from that day), so the republished text is dated 18 February 1998: the act as published wins over the day of the republication. Changes on that day are credited to Legea nr. 12/1998, which ordered the republication; files follow each text's own numbering.
- Legea nr. 26/1990: the portal holds no consolidated form between the republication of 1998 and the form of 14 June 2002, so the changes made in those years arrive with that form (save the repeal of art. 52, dated by its act).
- Legea nr. 26/1990: the acts that repealed arts. 52 (Legea nr. 348/2001), 49 (OUG nr. 129/2002) and 27 (Legea nr. 161/2003, book II, title II) were read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/29502, 38865, 43323). None sets an in-force day for those provisions, and before the revision of 2003 the Constitution (art. 78 of the 1991 text) put a law in force on its publication, so each repeal is dated by the day of publication, the day the portal's own notes give.
- Legea nr. 26/1990: the form of 21 April 2003 prints, after art. 53, art. III of OUG nr. 129/2002 (the transfer of the trade registry staff), introduced by a portal note ("Conform art. III ..."); it is not this law's text and is left out.
- Legea nr. 26/1990: the forms of 2003-2017 print the quotation marks of quoted terms in arts. 31 and 39 as "<<asociație familială>>" (which an HTML reader takes for a tag, losing the words) or as "lt; lt;asociație familială gt; gt;"; both are read as quoted terms („asociație familială”), so that no words are lost. Older notes spell "ORDONANTA" without diacritics; they are read as naming the ordinance.
- Legea nr. 26/1990: repealed in full by art. 140 alin. (1) lit. a) of Legea nr. 265/2022 privind registrul comerțului (enacted 22 July 2022, published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 750 of 26 July 2022), read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/257835), from the day that law took effect, 4 months after its publication (its art. 141 alin. (1)): 26 November 2022. The record ends with a version that removes every article, credited to Legea nr. 265/2022 (legea-265-2022/). The portal lists no form of Legea nr. 26/1990 after that of 26 September 2020, so any amendment between that day and the repeal is missing.
- Legea nr. 26/1990: the portal editorial notes printed as paragraphs of an article (on a Constitutional Court ruling, a rectification, a provision put back in force) are not text: 9 such paragraphs across all forms, for example «A se vedea anexa la ORDONANȚA DE URGENȚĂ nr. 44 din 16 april», «Conform alin. (4) al art. III din LEGEA nr. 441 din 27 noiem», «Conform art. II din LEGEA nr. 348 din 6 iulie 2001, publicat».
- Legea nr. 26/1990: articles a form leaves out are shown repealed on the day the repealing act took effect (on publication for an emergency ordinance, 3 days after for a law, unless the act sets its own day), credited to that act as its repeal note names it: art. 52, left out from the form of 14.06.2002: repealed by Legea nr. 348/2001, in force 12.07.2001 (no in-force clause of its own: in force on publication in Monitorul Oficial nr. 381 of 12 July 2001 (Constituția of 1991, art. 78)); art. 49, left out from the form of 11.10.2002: repealed by OUG nr. 129/2002, in force 11.10.2002 (no in-force clause of its own: in force on publication in Monitorul Oficial nr. 746 of 11 October 2002); art. 27, left out from the form of 21.04.2003: repealed by Legea nr. 161/2003, in force 21.04.2003 (book II, title II (art. VIII) has no in-force clause of its own (only book I titles II and V do, arts. 33 and 238): in force on publication in Monitorul Oficial nr. 279 of 21 April 2003 (Constituția of 1991, art. 78)).

- Legea nr. 263/2010: the newest form held is that of 31.08.2023; amendments in force after it may be missing.
- Legea nr. 263/2010: the first version is the law as published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 852 of 20 December 2010 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/124530), in force on 1 January 2011 (its art. 193 alin. (1)). Art. 70 alin. (1), art. 132, 135 and 194 took effect 3 days after publication (23 December 2010); they are shown from 1 January 2011 with the rest of the law.
- Legea nr. 263/2010: the portal's form of 30 December 2010 is the law with the changes of OUG nr. 117/2010 (Monitorul Oficial nr. 891 of 30 December 2010), published before the law took effect. It is read as of 1 January 2011, the same day as the text as published and after it, so the amended text is the one in force from that day; its changes are credited to OUG nr. 117/2010, as the portal's notes say. The form of 1 January 2011 adds only the portal's note quoting art. 17 of Legea nr. 287/2010 and changes no article.
- Legea nr. 263/2010: the portal lists one form (138601) under 1 October 2008, before the law existed. Its text is the law "updated to 24 June 2012" (the day the provisions of Decizia Curții Constituționale nr. 297/2012 ceased to have effect), and it sits between the forms of 9 May and 9 August 2012 in the portal's order of ids; it is read as of 24 June 2012. It changes no article.
- Legea nr. 263/2010: the forms of 20 July 2019 to 2021 print, on art. 149 alin. (2), the note "la 12-07-2021 ... abrogat de alin. (1) al art. 183 din LEGEA nr. 127 din 8 iulie 2019". The note on alin. (1) of the same article is dated 12-07-2019 and the portal's own note under both says that art. 183 alin. (1) took effect 3 days after publication (Monitorul Oficial nr. 563 of 9 July 2019), "respectiv 12 iulie 2019"; the printed year is a misprint and the note is read as 12 July 2019.
- Legea nr. 263/2010: the portal's notes on Decizia Curții Constituționale nr. 297/2012 (art. 196 lit. j), unconstitutional as it applies to the Court of Accounts' councillors) and nr. 680/2012 (art. 73 alin. (1), the phrase "în raport cu vârsta, conform tabelului nr. 3") say those provisions were suspended and then ceased (or would cease) to have effect on 24 June and 24 September 2012. No form removes their words on those days (art. 73 is rewritten by Legea nr. 37/2013 from 1 April 2013, and art. 196 lit. j) stays), so the text is kept as printed.
- Legea nr. 263/2010: the forms of 2011-2015 print, as paragraphs of arts. 27, 102, 125 and 170, the portal's quotations of Legea nr. 287/2010 art. 17 (contribution rates of 2011), OUG nr. 1/2013 art. II and Legea nr. 340/2013 art. II (the point value of 2013 and 2014), Legea nr. 6/2013 and Legea nr. 340/2013 art. 17 (death grant amounts) and the correction index of 2013; they are not the law's text and are left out.

- Legea nr. 51/1995: added 2026-11-02, one of the Portalul legislativ's consolidated forms per version, as for the other acts: 38 pages read from Internet Archive captures, each pinned in forms.json and in its version's source_url. A capture was used only if it was complete and the page showed the form its document id stands for.
- Legea nr. 51/1995: the newest form held is that of 10.05.2024 (the act page's capture 20260221233038 lists no later form); amendments in force after it may be missing.
- Legea nr. 51/1995: the republication of 06.03.2001 (27141) has no captured page of its own that shows it, so it is read from DetaliiDocumentAfis/27141, the republished text as printed in the Monitorul Oficial (capture 20260311183307); that page carries no amendment notes.
- Legea nr. 51/1995: the republication of 07.02.2011 (126008) has no captured page of its own that shows it, so it is read from DetaliiDocumentAfis/126008, the republished text as printed in the Monitorul Oficial (capture 20260225152905); that page carries no amendment notes.
- Legea nr. 51/1995: the republication of 24.05.2018 (201006) has no captured page of its own that shows it, so it is read from DetaliiDocumentAfis/201006, the republished text as printed in the Monitorul Oficial (capture 20260614093224); that page carries no amendment notes.
- Legea nr. 51/1995: Decizia CCR nr. 109/2010 read as published: it admits the exception and finds the provisions of OUG nr. 159/2008 unconstitutional (art. 115 alin. (4) of the Constitution); art. I pct. 30 of that ordinance inserted art. 68^14. The form of 03.05.2010 still prints it, with the portal's note that it ceased to have effect from 3 May 2010.
- Legea nr. 51/1995: Legea nr. 270/2010 read as published: art. I pct. 41: "Articolul 77 se abrogă."; pct. 8: "Articolul 16^1 se abrogă."; pct. 38-39 insert arts. 68^1-68^13 (no art. 68^14); art. VI orders the republication with renumbered articles.
- Legea nr. 51/1995: Legea nr. 280/2004 read as published: articol unic pct. 1-2: approving OUG nr. 77/2003, it rewrites the ordinance's title and its art. I so that the ordinance inserts art. 82^1 into Legea nr. 161/2003 instead of art. 44^1 into Legea nr. 51/1995 ("După articolul 82 din Legea nr. 161/2003 ... se introduce un articol nou, articolul 82^1"); art. 44^1 of Legea nr. 51/1995 (inserted by OUG nr. 77/2003 art. I, Monitorul Oficial nr. 640 of 9 September 2003) leaves the law that day. The form of 02.07.2004 prints it as a bare "ART. 44^1 *)" paragraph inside art. 44.
- Legea nr. 51/1995: OUG nr. 159/2008 read as published: art. I pct. 8: "Articolul 16^1 se abrogă."; pct. 30 rewrites chapter V and inserts arts. 68^1-68^14 (art. 68^14: "U.N.B.R. şi barourile conlucrează cu Ministerul Justiţiei ...").
- Legea nr. 51/1995: the base text is missing. The portal's base form (DetaliiDocumentAfis/282141) shows the law as republished in 2018, not the 1995 text. The record starts at the oldest form held, the law as republished in Monitorul Oficial nr. 113 of 6 March 2001 under art. V of Legea nr. 231/2000 (enacted 4 December 2000, published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 635 of 7 December 2000), credited to the publisher as the oldest version available; that text already holds the changes made by OUG nr. 85/1997 and Legea nr. 231/2000, as its republication note says. No enacted version is made up; the law's enactment (7 June 1995) and first publication (9 June 1995) are kept in its metadata.
- Legea nr. 51/1995: the portal holds no consolidated form between the republication of 6 March 2001 and 9 September 2003, so the changes made in between (Legea nr. 489/2002) arrive together that day.
- Legea nr. 51/1995: the republications of 7 February 2011 (art. VI of Legea nr. 270/2010, enacted 22 December 2010, published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 872 of 28 December 2010) and 24 May 2018 (art. II of Legea nr. 25/2017, enacted 24 March 2017, published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 210 of 28 March 2017), each with renumbered articles, are read from their own pages (DetaliiDocumentAfis/126008 and 201006); changes on those days that no note explains are credited to the law that ordered the republication, as each republication note says; files follow each text's own numbering, so many change those days.
- Legea nr. 51/1995: art. 44^1, inserted by OUG nr. 77/2003 (in force on publication, 9 September 2003), leaves the law on 2 July 2004: Legea nr. 280/2004, approving the ordinance, moved its text into Legea nr. 161/2003 as art. 82^1 (read as published). The form of 2 July 2004 prints it as a bare paragraph "ART. 44^1 *)" inside art. 44, which is filed as its own article and then left out; the forms of 3 September 2004 to 25 August 2006 still print its text, and those of 18 March and 13 May 2010 print it as "Eliminat." with the portal's note; these prints are the publisher's error and are left out, so the article stays out from 2 July 2004 (coordinator ruling).
- Legea nr. 51/1995: art. 68^14, inserted by OUG nr. 159/2008 (26 November 2008), ceases to have effect on 3 May 2010 under Decizia Curții Constituționale nr. 109/2010 (Monitorul Oficial nr. 175 of 18 March 2010), which found the whole ordinance unconstitutional: suspended from 18 March to 2 May 2010, ceased from 3 May 2010 (Constituția art. 147 alin. (1); coordinator ruling). The forms of 3 May 2010 (text) and 13 May 2010 ("Eliminat.") still print it and are left out for it. Legea nr. 81/2010, which rejected OUG nr. 159/2008 and on which the portal's "Eliminat." note on art. 68^14 relies, came after the cessation and is not credited for that article.
- Legea nr. 51/1995: the other changes of OUG nr. 159/2008 are shown as the portal's forms print them: the forms of 18 March and 3 May 2010 still print the ordinance's wording, with the portal's notes that it was found unconstitutional, suspended and then ceased (those notes, printed as paragraphs, are left out); the form of 13 May 2010 prints the earlier wording again, or "Eliminat." for the provisions the ordinance had inserted, and its notes name Legea nr. 81/2010, which is credited with those changes.
- Legea nr. 51/1995: art. 16^1 (inserted by Legea nr. 255/2004) is left out by the form of 26 November 2008 and dated as repealed that day by OUG nr. 159/2008 art. I pct. 8 (the portal's note; the ordinance read as published). The forms of 18 March and 13 May 2010 print it as "Abrogat." with the portal's notes that the repeal was suspended and then ceased, and, in the second, that art. 16^1 had already ceased to have effect under Decizia Curții Constituționale nr. 513/2006 (Monitorul Oficial nr. 598 of 11 July 2006); the forms of 2006-2008 print its text, so the record follows them. Arts. 31^1 and 37^1, repealed in 2004 and 2005 and printed as "Abrogat.", are left out by some forms of 2008-2010 and carried as repealed.
- Legea nr. 51/1995: the form of 26 November 2008 prints art. 68^10 run into art. 68^9 ("Art. 68^10. - (1) Pentru înscrierea ..."); it is filed as its own article. Its note is printed on art. 68^9, so art. 68^10 is not credited that day and is committed as the publisher's (OUG nr. 159/2008 art. I pct. 30 inserted it).
- Legea nr. 51/1995: from the form of 26 November 2008 the portal reprints the older undated notes in new wording ("Art. 1 a fost modificat de pct. 1 al art. I din LEGEA nr. 255 din 16 iunie 2004, publicată ..." for "Art. 1 a fost modificat de LEGEA nr. 255 din 16 iunie 2004 publicată ..."); a reworded note naming a law already noted on that article is the same note, so the typesetting changes printed with it are committed as the publisher's, not credited to that law again.
- Legea nr. 51/1995: some forms print an article as an empty article heading inside the article before it (or as a bare paragraph "ART. 29 *)"), followed by its text as paragraphs of that article; the text from the heading on is filed as the article it names: Articolul 44^1, Articolul 68^10.
- Legea nr. 51/1995: the portal editorial notes printed as paragraphs of an article (on a Constitutional Court ruling, a rectification, a provision put back in force) are not text: 232 such paragraphs across all forms, for example «(la 10-07-2022 Litera a) din Articolul 14, Sectiunea 1, Capi», «* Fost art. 84, introdus prin Legea nr. 255/2004.», «A se vedea și art. II din LEGEA nr. 201 din 25 mai 2004 publ».
- Legea nr. 51/1995: the portal's notes that a provision went back to its earlier wording, or was removed, because the emergency ordinance that changed it was rejected by a law are read as notes naming that law, which is credited with the change (such notes are printed from the form of 13.05.2010).
- Legea nr. 51/1995: articles a form leaves out are shown repealed on the day the repealing act took effect (on publication for an emergency ordinance, 3 days after for a law, unless the act sets its own day), credited to that act as its repeal note names it: art. 44^1, left out from the form of 02.07.2004: repealed by Legea nr. 280/2004, in force 02.07.2004 (a law with no in-force clause of its own: 3 days after publication in Monitorul Oficial nr. 574 of 29 June 2004 (Constituția art. 78)); art. 16^1, left out from the form of 26.11.2008: repealed by OUG nr. 159/2008, in force 26.11.2008 (an emergency ordinance with no general in-force clause of its own (its art. III and IV defer only the provisions on professional limited-liability companies and art. I pct. 6): in force on publication in Monitorul Oficial nr. 792 of 26 November 2008); art. 68^14, left out from the form of 03.05.2010: repealed by Decizia CCR nr. 109/2010, in force 03.05.2010 (a Constitutional Court decision published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 175 of 18 March 2010: the provisions it finds unconstitutional are suspended for 45 days from publication (18 March - 2 May 2010) and, none having been brought into line, cease to have effect from 3 May 2010 (Constituția art. 147 alin. (1))); art. 77, left out from the form of 31.12.2010: repealed by Legea nr. 270/2010, in force 31.12.2010 (a law with no in-force clause of its own: 3 days after publication in Monitorul Oficial nr. 872 of 28 December 2010 (Constituția art. 78)).
- Legea nr. 51/1995: forms that leave out an article already shown repealed ("Abrogat") change nothing in the law, so its "Abrogat" text is kept through them: art. 31^1 through the form(s) of 26.11.2008; art. 37^1 through the form(s) of 26.11.2008; art. 16^1 through the form(s) of 03.05.2010; art. 31^1 through the form(s) of 03.05.2010; art. 37^1 through the form(s) of 03.05.2010; art. 16^1 through the form(s) of 31.12.2010; art. 31^1 through the form(s) of 31.12.2010; art. 37^1 through the form(s) of 31.12.2010.

- Legea nr. 51/1995: forms with no change after normalisation, so no version: 2006-08-25 (78957), 2012-01-27 (137386), 2012-03-13 (137387), 2017-06-22 (189841), 2017-07-06 (191892), 2022-05-26 (255263), 2024-05-10 (126008).
- Legea nr. 71/2011: added 2026-11-02, one of the Portalul legislativ's consolidated forms per version, as for the other acts. A capture was used only if it was complete and the page showed the form its document id stands for. The forms are those listed by DetaliiDocument/129268 as captured on 2025-10-17, the newest capture of any of the act's pages: 12 forms, 12 held.
- Legea nr. 71/2011: the newest form held is that of 16.07.2025; amendments in force after it may be missing.
- Legea nr. 71/2011: the law as published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 409 of 10 June 2011 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/129268). Its art. 221 puts it in force with the Civil Code, on 1 October 2011 (art. 220 alin. (1)), except arts. 214, 216-218, 224, art. 225 alin. (1) and (2), arts. 226 and 228, in force 3 days after publication. So the first version, of 13 June 2011, holds only those provisions; on 1 October 2011 the rest of the law as published is added, credited to Legea nr. 71/2011.
- Legea nr. 71/2011: the portal's form of 8 July 2011 comes before the day the law took effect save its early provisions, and changes none of them: it prints the corrections of RECTIFICAREA published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 489 of 8 July 2011 to arts. 20, 83, 99, 190 and 212. It is not used; those corrections arrive with the form of 1 October 2011, credited to that rectification from its notes.
- Legea nr. 71/2011: most of its articles amend other acts and quote the text they insert or rewrite ("Articolul 1 va avea următorul cuprins: ..."). The quoted text is kept inside the amending article, as printed, and is not filed as articles of this law. In later forms the portal rewrites some quoted text as the amended act changed (art. 90, art. 5 of Legea nr. 341/2004, by OUG nr. 95/2014) and adds a note naming that act; such a change is credited from the note when the note dates it after the form before, and is otherwise the publisher's.
- Legea nr. 71/2011: the form of 4 February 2016 reprints the quoted articles in the portal's newer layout ("Articolul 1" for "ART. 1", without the quotation marks) and with the later wording of some amended acts, and several forms of 2013-2014 move line breaks around the names of other acts; no note covers these changes, so they are the publisher's, labelled unexplained.
- Legea nr. 71/2011: a note the portal reprints with new spelling ("Denumirea marginala" / "marginală", art. 83, form of 19 October 2014) is not a new note, so the typesetting change printed with it is not credited to the act it names.

- Legea nr. 76/2012: the law as published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 365 of 30 May 2012 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/138484). Its art. 82 puts it in force on the day its art. 81 alin. (1) gives the Code of Civil Procedure, except art. 80, in force 3 days after publication. That day was 1 September 2012 as published; OUG nr. 44/2012 moved it to 1 February 2013 and OUG nr. 4/2013 to 15 February 2013, each before the earlier day came. So the first version, of 2 June 2012, holds only art. 80; on 15 February 2013 the rest of the law as published is added, credited to Legea nr. 76/2012, and the same day the portal's form of 15.02.2013 gives the text in force (OUG nr. 44/2012, OUG nr. 4/2013, and Legea nr. 2/2013, which repealed arts. 39 and 60 from that day).
- Legea nr. 76/2012: the portal's form of 14 February 2013 comes before the day the law took effect (save art. 80, which it does not change) and is not used; its changes (OUG nr. 44/2012, to art. 81) arrive with the form of 15 February 2013.
- Legea nr. 76/2012: art. 82 as amended by OUG nr. 4/2013 put art. 38 pct. 1 and 2 (the new arts. 12 and 13 of Legea nr. 193/2000) in force on 1 July 2013, and Legea nr. 214/2013 moved that to 1 October 2013. Under the rule for text printed before its day, those two points are absent until 1 October 2013 and are added that day, credited to Legea nr. 76/2012.
- Legea nr. 76/2012: most of its articles amend other acts and quote the text they insert or rewrite. The quoted text is kept inside the amending article, as printed. The law as published prints art. 13's quoted arts. 964^1 and 964^2 of the Code of Civil Procedure as paragraphs ("Art. 964^1"), and the forms print them as articles placed after art. 13, the rest of art. 13 inside the second; both are read as text of art. 13, in the printed order.

- OUG nr. 44/2008: the newest form held is that of 26.11.2022; amendments in force after it may be missing.
- OUG nr. 44/2008: the first version is the emergency ordinance as published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 328 of 25 April 2008 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/91808), dated 25 April 2008: it sets no in-force day of its own, so in force on publication. Its annex (Anexa 1, the documents supporting an application for registration) is not an article and is not read; the portal's consolidated forms do not print it.
- OUG nr. 44/2008: approved with amendments by Legea nr. 182/2016 (Monitorul Oficial nr. 828 of 19 October 2016); the portal's notes date its changes 17 January 2017, the date of the form that carries them, and each change is credited to it from those notes.
- OUG nr. 44/2008: the portal prints the repeal notes of arts. 34 and 42 just after the article ("Articolul 34 ... a fost abrogat de Punctul 27, Articolul I din LEGEA nr. 182 ..."); they are read as that article's notes. The signature block printed after art. 44 ("PRIM-MINISTRU ...") is not part of the article.
- OUG nr. 44/2008: 4 changed article(s) with no note naming a law, committed as the publisher's: 2017-01-17 art-043, 2022-05-09 art-003, 2022-05-09 art-042, 2022-11-26 art-034.
- OUG nr. 50/2010: added 2026-11-02, one of the Portalul legislativ's consolidated forms per version, as for the other acts. A capture was used only if it was complete and the page showed the form its document id stands for. The forms are those listed by DetaliiDocument/124969 as captured on 2026-01-25, the newest capture of any of the act's pages: 8 forms, 8 held.
- OUG nr. 50/2010: the newest form held is that of 17.03.2024; amendments in force after it may be missing.
- OUG nr. 50/2010: the first version is the emergency ordinance as published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 389 of 11 June 2010 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/119472), dated 21 June 2010: in force within 10 days of publication (its art. 94). The portal's consolidated forms start on 2 January 2011, so the changes made before that day arrive together with that form.
- OUG nr. 50/2010: the portal prints repeal and change notes just after the article they concern; they are read as that article's notes. The signature block printed after the last article is not part of the article.
- OUG nr. 50/2010: art. 92 amends Legea nr. 190/1999 and quotes the articles it rewrites; the quoted articles are read as part of art. 92. The portal prints some of those quotations differently from one form to the next (with or without quotation marks, "Articolul 8" repeated as a heading), and the text of arts. 41, 49, 53, 66, 70, 92, 95^1 and 96 differs between forms in typing corrections, a dropped transposition footnote (art. 96) and joined words ("art. 7pct. 5"): no note names a law for them, so they are committed as the publisher's. Its annexes (Anexele nr. 1-3, the information forms) are not articles and are not read.
- OUG nr. 50/2010: 8 changed article(s) with no note naming a law, committed as the publisher's: 2016-09-30 art-041, 2016-09-30 art-053, 2016-09-30 art-066, 2016-09-30 art-096, 2019-05-02 art-049, 2019-05-02 art-070, 2019-05-02 art-092, 2019-05-02 art-095-1.
- OUG nr. 50/2010: forms with no change after normalisation, so no version: 2013-01-01 (143372).
- OUG nr. 77/2014: added 2026-11-02, one of the Portalul legislativ's consolidated forms per version, as for the other acts. A capture was used only if it was complete and the page showed the form its document id stands for. The forms are those listed by DetaliiDocumentAfis/163659 as captured on 2026-01-18, the newest capture of any of the act's pages: 7 forms, 7 held.
- OUG nr. 77/2014: the newest form held is that of 29.07.2024; amendments in force after it may be missing.
- OUG nr. 77/2014: the first version is the emergency ordinance as published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 893 of 9 December 2014 (DetaliiDocumentAfis/163659), dated 1 January 2015, when its arts. 1-49 take effect (its art. 51 alin. (1)). Arts. 50 (which amends Legea concurenței nr. 21/1996) and 51 took effect on publication, 9 December 2014; they are shown from 1 January 2015 with the rest.
- OUG nr. 77/2014: the portal's form of 29 December 2014 is the text as rectified in Monitorul Oficial nr. 954 of 29 December 2014, published before arts. 1-49 took effect. It is read as of 1 January 2015, the same day as the text as published and after it, so the rectified text is the one in force from that day; its changes are credited to the rectification, as the portal's notes say.
- OUG nr. 77/2014: art. 50 quotes the articles of Legea nr. 21/1996 it rewrites; the quoted articles are kept as art. 50's text.
- OUG nr. 77/2014: 6 changed article(s) with no note naming a law, committed as the publisher's: 2015-01-01 art-050, 2015-01-01 art-051, 2015-03-09 art-030, 2015-03-13 art-030, 2015-03-13 art-050, 2015-03-13 art-051.

## What AI did and did not do

The scripts that read the sources and build this repository were written by Claude Code running Claude Opus 5.5 (`claude-opus-5-5`) under Lexifina's direction.

No model wrote, translated or corrected legal text. No model runs at build time. Every word of law in this repository comes from the official sources above.

The commit messages are written by `build.py` from the source data.

Model use at data-build time:
- Claude Code with Claude Opus 5.5 (claude-opus-5-5) wrote Lexifina's code and the README; no model output is used as legal text, and no model runs at data-build or git-build time.
- Codul de procedură civilă: parsed by Lexifina's code, which Claude Code with Claude Opus 5.5 (claude-opus-5-5) extended for Phase 2; no model output is used as legal text.
